[048] t_030aacca5a8b7062 dispatch.when
{"id": "t_030aacca5a8b7062", "family": "dispatch.when", "text": "Let U be the engine chosen by Model initialization. U is umma when the device-0 major capability queried by _Engines is 10 and the UMMA library builds and loads successfully; otherwise initialization tries the sync engine. A binding-module failure alone retains UMMA via ctypes. Let S be the device-0 multiprocessor count passed by that wrapper. Let P be the engine selector returned by sched_policy in the UMMA module actually invoked. Its cache key is (address(m_indptr), E, M, N, K). A cache hit reuses P, without deriving it again from current tensor contents. On a miss, the host attempts a device-to-host pointer-array copy and stream synchronization. P defaults to 0 if either fails or the copied boundaries are not nonnegative, nondecreasing and at most M. After a successful read, let h be the copied boundaries, A = count of integers 0 <= g < E with h[g+1] > h[g], T = sum over 0 <= g < E of ceil((h[g+1]-h[g])/128), B = K/128 and I = T*(N/256). Let W mean N is divisible by 256, A > 0, S > 0, and at least one of: (B >= 32 and ((I >= 4*S and T >= 4*A) or (B <= 48 and 2*I >= 5*S and T >= A))); (B >= 16 and I >= 8*S and (T >= 2*A or T == A)); (8 <= B <= 12 and I >= 10*S and T >= A); (8 <= B <= 20 and I >= 4*S and T >= 2*A). The new selector is P=0 when W is false, P=2 when W is true and B <= 20 and 2*T >= 3*A, and P=1 otherwise. The normal build sets sareg=0. Let L be this request's actual compute-kernel submissions, including any submission preceding an API-error fallback. Graph construction and instantiation alone add no computation to L. For a recorded request, use its recorded host selection and by-value arguments at replay; device reads still use the tensor contents at execution time. Let F denote umma_wide<4,32768,0> and G denote umma_wide<3,65536,1>. Select exactly when U is umma, P=2, and L is exactly [G,G,F,F]. These are four actual compute submissions in one request, ordered by the same stream. G starts while g_w1g_off is false. Its graph replay reports an error after submitting computation, then direct G submits and reports an error, setting g_w1g_off and continuing to F. F's graph replay also reports an error after submitting computation, then direct F submits the fourth computation and returns its status.
